#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)

The OpenMP build passes 60 comparisons against the original, including exact result hashes at 1, 2, 4, and 8 threads. That covers odd and even iteration counts, non-cubic grids, and grids with no interior cells. I’m now measuring scaling and checking whether cache blocking improves throughput.

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import os, pathlib, re, statistics, subprocess, time
work = pathlib.Path('/tmp/stencil3d-check-tlj6bt34')
source = pathlib.Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp').read_text()
start = source.index('    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)')
end = source.index('\\n}\\n\\nbool validateResult', start)
body = source[start:end]
for tile_z, tile_y in ((8,8),(16,16)):
    tiled = body.replace('for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\\n        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {',
       f'for (size_t zb = 1; zb < nz - 1; zb += {tile_z}) {{\\n        for (size_t yb = 1; yb < ny - 1; yb += {tile_y}) {{\\n            const size_t zend = std::min(zb + {tile_z}, nz - 1);\\n            const size_t yend = std::min(yb + {tile_y}, ny - 1);\\n            for (size_t z = zb; z < zend; ++z) {{\\n                for (size_t y = yb; y < yend; ++y) {{') + '\\n        }\\n    }'
    variant = work / f'tile{tile_z}'
    variant.mkdir()
    subprocess.run(['c++','-std=c++20','-O3','-march=native','-fopenmp','-Wall','-Wextra','-I',str(pathlib.Path('stencil3d').resolve()),'-x','c++','-','-o',str(variant/'stencil3d')],input=source[:start]+tiled+source[end:],text=True,check=True)
print('Cache-blocked variants compiled.',flush=True)
for size, iterations in ((128,300),(256,80),(512,20)):
    for variant in ('original','openmp','tile8','tile16'):
        for threads in ((1,) if variant == 'original' else (1,8,32,64)):
            env = dict(os.environ,OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads),OMP_PROC_BIND='spread',OMP_PLACES='cores')
            values=[]
            for repeat in range(2):
                result=subprocess.run([str(work/variant/'stencil3d'),'-x',str(size),'-i',str(iterations)],env=env,text=True,capture_output=True,check=True)
                values.append(float(re.search(r'Performance: ([\\d.]+)',result.stdout).group(1)))
            print(f'{size}"'^3 x {iterations}: {variant:8} {threads:2} threads: {statistics.median(values):9.3f} MCellUpdates/s {values}'"',flush=True)

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
template <typename T>
struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
    template <typename U>
    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };

    template <typename U>
    void construct(U* ptr) {
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
    }
};

using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;

void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
        grid[idx] = value;
        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
    }
}

// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. The worksharing barrier
// completes all output writes before the next iteration reads that buffer.
void stencilIteration(const Real* input, Real* output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) return;

    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            const size_t row = z * plane + y * nx;
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = row + x;
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx - 1];
                const Real right = input[idx + 1];
                const Real front = input[idx - nx];
                const Real back = input[idx + nx];
                const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // Check finiteness and the value range in a single parallel pass.
    int nonFinite = 0;
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:nonFinite) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
        const Real val = grid[idx];
        nonFinite |= std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    if (nonFinite) {
        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    Grid grid1(gridSize);
    Grid grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, grid2, iterations);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        const Real* input = grid1.data();
        Real* output = grid2.data();
        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            stencilIteration(input, output, nx, ny, nz);
            // Each worker swaps its own pointers after the worksharing barrier.
            input = output;
            output = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid1.data() : grid2.data();
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2 && iterations > 0)
        ? static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
    double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    double mcups = cellUpdates / seconds / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const Grid& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The row-based version scales well on 256³: about 37 billion cell updates/s at 64 threads, versus 447 million for the original serial code. The 512³ case shows signs of memory-bandwidth limits, so I’m finishing the cache-blocking comparison before choosing the final loop layout.

+// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. The worksharing barrier
+// completes all output writes before the next iteration reads that buffer.
+void stencilIteration(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());
+    const size_t tileZ = 16;
+    // Keep the working set of three input strips and one output strip small
+    // enough for private caches, while retaining contiguous SIMD rows.
+    const size_t tileY = std::min(ny - 2, std::max<size_t>(1, 8192 / nx));
+    const size_t tiles = ((nz - 3) / tileZ + 1) * ((ny - 3) / tileY + 1);
+    if (plane >= 131072 && threads >= 16 && tiles >= threads) {
+        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; z += tileZ) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; y += tileY) {
+                stencilRows(input, output, nx, plane,
+                            z, std::min(z + tileZ, nz - 1),
+                            y, std::min(y + tileY, ny - 1));
             }
         }
+    } else {
+        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
+                stencilRows(input, output, nx, plane, z, z + 1, y, y + 1);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. The worksharing barrier
+// completes all output writes before the next iteration reads that buffer.
+void stencilIteration(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());
+    const size_t tileZ = 16;
+    // Keep the working set of three input strips and one output strip small
+    // enough for private caches, while retaining contiguous SIMD rows.
+    const size_t tileY = std::min(ny - 2, std::max<size_t>(1, 8192 / nx));
+    const size_t tiles = ((nz - 3) / tileZ + 1) * ((ny - 3) / tileY + 1);
+    if (plane >= 131072 && threads >= 16 && tiles >= threads) {
+        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; z += tileZ) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; y += tileY) {
+                stencilRows(input, output, nx, plane,
+                            z, std::min(z + tileZ, nz - 1),
+                            y, std::min(y + tileY, ny - 1));
             }
         }
+    } else {
+        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
+                stencilRows(input, output, nx, plane, z, z + 1, y, y + 1);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }

codex
Cache blocking improved 512³ throughput at 64 threads by about 37%. I’ve retained row-based worksharing for smaller grids and added blocking for large planes. Wide, shallow grids can also split work within rows. All paths use OpenMP and preserve the original floating-point operation order.

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRows(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t plane,
+                        const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                        const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd) {
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            stencilRow(input, output, nx, plane, z * plane + y * nx, 1, nx - 1);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }

+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }

codex
All 126 comparisons and 27 AddressSanitizer/UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer runs pass. The final kernel reaches about 38.3 billion updates/s on 256³ with 64 threads—roughly 86× the original here. I also found that the small default grid slows down when OpenMP launches all 256 logical CPUs. I’ll limit that default team size while preserving explicit `OMP_NUM_THREADS` settings.

+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+int gridThreadCount(const size_t gridSize) {
+    const int maximum = omp_get_max_threads();
+    if (std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS")) return maximum;
+
+    // Small volumes cannot amortize large-team synchronization. Without
+    // affinity, leave more work per worker to reduce scheduling overhead.
+    const size_t cellsPerThread = omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false
+        ? 131072 : 32768;
+    const size_t workers = std::max<size_t>(1, gridSize / cellsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(maximum, workers));
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations, const int threads) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(threads) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }

 using Real = double;
 
-// 3D index calculation
-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
-    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+int gridThreadCount(const size_t gridSize) {
+    const int maximum = omp_get_max_threads();
+    if (std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS")) return maximum;
+
+    // Small volumes cannot amortize large-team synchronization. Without
+    // affinity, leave more work per worker to reduce scheduling overhead.
+    const size_t cellsPerThread = omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false
+        ? 131072 : 32768;
+    const size_t workers = std::max<size_t>(1, gridSize / cellsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(maximum, workers));
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations, const int threads) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(threads) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }

-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
-    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+int gridThreadCount(const size_t gridSize) {
+    const int maximum = omp_get_max_threads();
+    if (std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS")) return maximum;
+
+    // Small volumes cannot amortize large-team synchronization. Without
+    // affinity, leave more work per worker to reduce scheduling overhead.
+    const size_t cellsPerThread = omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false
+        ? 131072 : 32768;
+    const size_t workers = std::max<size_t>(1, gridSize / cellsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(maximum, workers));
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations, const int threads) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(threads) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }

-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
-    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
+// Leave storage untouched until parallel initialization, so each worker first
+// touches its own pages on NUMA machines instead of placing them all on one CPU.
+template <typename T>
+struct UninitializedAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = UninitializedAllocator<U>; };
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U;
+    }
+};
+
+using Grid = std::vector<Real, UninitializedAllocator<Real>>;
+
+int gridThreadCount(const size_t gridSize) {
+    const int maximum = omp_get_max_threads();
+    if (std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS")) return maximum;
+
+    // Small volumes cannot amortize large-team synchronization. Without
+    // affinity, leave more work per worker to reduce scheduling overhead.
+    const size_t cellsPerThread = omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false
+        ? 131072 : 32768;
+    const size_t workers = std::max<size_t>(1, gridSize / cellsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(maximum, workers));
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& scratch, const int iterations, const int threads) {
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(threads) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
+        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
+        grid[idx] = value;
+        // Boundaries never change. Seed both buffers once instead of scanning
+        // the whole volume to copy boundaries after every iteration.
+        scratch[idx] = iterations > 0 ? value : 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        const Real center = input[idx];
+        const Real left = input[idx - 1];
+        const Real right = input[idx + 1];
+        const Real front = input[idx - nx];
+        const Real back = input[idx + nx];
+        const Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+
+        // Preserve the original operation order and division.
+        output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+    }
 }
